Travelers is tab 0 and Report is tab 2, so swiping Travelers → Report makes the adapter **destroy and detach the `ParticipantTabActivity` fragment**.\n\nThe contextual action bar started by long-clicking a traveler is however owned by the *host activity* (`TrickyTripperActivity.mActionMode`) and is never finished on that swipe. The still-visible CAB keeps dispatching clicks into the detached fragment\'s inner `MyActionModeCallback`, where:\n\n```java\nprivate TrickyTripperApp getApp() {\n    return (TrickyTripperApp) getActivity().getApplication();  // line 96 — getActivity() == null\n}\n```\n\n`getActivity()` returns `null` once detached → exactly the reported NPE (`ParticipantTabActivity.java:96` called from `:36` / `$b.a(:154)`, i.e. `onActionItemClicked` → `getApp()`).\n\n## The fix (3 files)\n\n**`ParticipantTabActivity.java`** — the actual crash site:\n- Cache the `TrickyTripperApp` in a field, resolved in `onAttach(Context)` (lazily re-resolved in `getApp()`). The `Application` outlives the fragment, so the menu action keeps working instead of dereferencing a null activity. `ViewControllerImpl` is already constructed with the application context and adds `FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK`, so `openCreatePayment` works correctly from a detached state.\n- `onActionItemClicked` now bails out with `mode.finish()` if the selection or app is unavailable.\n- `option_participant_show_report` null-checks the activity/`ViewPager` before navigating.\n- The delete branch uses the app context for `Toast`/`getResources()` (`getResources()` also blows up on a detached fragment).\n- `getRunningActionMode()` / `setRunningActionMode()` are null-safe via a new `getHostActivity()` helper using an `instanceof` check.\n\n**`TrickyTripperActivity.java`** — prevents a follow-on regression: since a detached fragment can no longer clear the activity\'s `mActionMode`, a stale non-null value would permanently block all future long-click action modes (all the handlers early-return on `mActionMode != null`). Overriding `onSupportActionModeFinished` guarantees the field is reset by the owner.\n\n**`PaymentTabActivity.java`** — carries the identical pattern (`getApp()` via `getActivity()`, unguarded CAB accessors); hardened the same way so the bug class can\'t resurface there on recreation paths.\n\nExisting behaviour is otherwise untouched: while the fragment is attached everything resolves exactly as before, and the "create payment" action now performs its intended work rather than crashing.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 131, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 347, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 64780, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 347, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAa5AFEj3xi9vYauDxLs', stop_reason=None, session_id='4bef004f-d3f4-4018-9830-a9873df44114', uuid='1c771d06-8005-4a5e-8984-562209bbf75f')
